Vendor's own pages: Carlson comparison blog (May 2026) "Carlson Survey is $2,350 — once", OEM $3,450, maintenance "optional, roughly 10% of the original price": carlson-us.com/product-comparisons/carlson-su… ; Carlson customer portal, Civil Suite unit price $4,450.00 USD: account.carlsonsw.com/purchaseproducts ; Carlson Preferred Solutions store, Survey IntelliCAD 2027 $2,349.95 "new, end user perpetual license", price valid until 2026-10-25: carlsonps.com/products/carlson-survey-intellicad ; Civil Suite 2026 $4,450.00 (Add Maintenance +$445): carlsonps.com/products/carlson-civil-suite . Independent dealers: Carlson Survey Supply, Survey w/ iCAD $2,350, OEM $3,450, 1-yr maintenance $235, "perpetual license without annual fees": carlsonsurveysupply.com/product/carlson-surve… ; maintenance = 10% of list, +10% per lapsed year: carlsonsurveysupply.com/carlson-software-main… ; Civil Suite 1-yr maintenance $445: carlsonsurveysupply.com/product/carlson-civil… ; AAI Survey Civil Suite $4,450: aaisurvey.com/products/carlson-civil-suite-so… ; FLT Geosystems Civil Suite 2025 $4,450: secure.fltgeosystems.com/cgi-local/products.c… . Outlier: Geo-Tronics $3,750 (listing ~628 days old): geo-tronics.com/product/carlson-civil-suite/
research:market-and-stabilityeTransmit does not automatically include SHX/TTF fonts, .pat, .lin, .pmp, OLE links or URL-referenced files.holds highCorrect, with two precisions. (1) The cited "Transmittal Package File Reference" (AutoCAD 2027) lists under "File Types Not Added Automatically": *.shx, *.ttf, *.pfa/*.pfb (PostScript Type 1 fonts), *.pat, *.lin, *.pmp, and "OLE objects (linked files)". It does not mention URL-referenced files; that part comes from a different Autodesk page, the Files Tree / Files Table tab reference for the Create Transmittal dialog: "Related files that are referenced by URLs are not included in the transmittal package." (2) Fonts are a default-off option rather than impossible to include: the Standard transmittal setup omits them, but the Modify Transmittal Setup dialog's "Include Fonts" checkbox adds TTF and SHX files to the package (Autodesk KB: "The default transmittal configuration does not include fonts"). By contrast .pat, .lin, .pmp, linked OLE files and URL-referenced files have no include option and must be added manually via Add File. Note the asymmetry on plotter files: PC3 (and CTB/STB) ARE added automatically; only the companion PMP is not. A precise wording: "By default (Standard setup) eTransmit does not include SHX, TTF or PostScript fonts (these can be added with the Include Fonts option), and it never automatically adds .pat, .lin, .pmp, linked OLE files, or files referenced by URL."1) Cited source, confirmed verbatim through the Autodesk Product Help search index (AutoCAD 2027, "Transmittal Package File Reference"): help.autodesk.com/view/ACD/2027/PTB/?guid=GUI… — "File Types Not Added Automatically: *.shx Compiled shape files used for text styles or complex linetypes; *.ttf True Type font files used for text styles; *.pfa, *.pfb PostScript Type 1 font files; *.pat Hatch pattern files; *.lin Linetype definition files; *.pmp Plotter model parameter files used to store plotter configuration information; OLE objects (linked files) OLE objects that are linked to files in the drawing." Same table in the "Added Automatically" list includes *.pc3, *.ctb, *.stb. Same table appears in the 2016/2018/2019/2021/2023/2026 cloudhelp editions (e.g. help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2021/ENU/AutoCAD-…, help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2026/ENU/AutoCAD-…). 2) Independent Autodesk page, Files Tree Tab (Create Transmittal Dialog Box), AutoCAD 2027: help.autodesk.com/view/ACD/2027/PTB/?guid=GUI… and Files Table Tab: help.autodesk.com/view/ACD/2027/PTB/?guid=GUI… — "Related files that are referenced by URLs are not included in the transmittal package." 3) Modify Transmittal Setup Dialog Box, AutoCAD 2027: help.autodesk.com/view/ACD/2027/PTB/?guid=GUI… — "Include Fonts: Includes any associated font files (TTF and SHX) with the transmittal package." 4) Autodesk support article "Fonts are not included in ZIP file created by eTransmit in AutoCAD": autodesk.com/support/technical/article/caas/s… — "The default transmittal configuration does not include fonts." 5) "Package Drawings for Transmittal" (AutoCAD 2027): help.autodesk.com/view/ACD/2027/ENU/?guid=GUI… — automatically added: CTB/STB, PC3, external references; "Files that might be identified and excluded by default are font or unloaded reference files." 6) Older vendor doc via search snippet, AutoCAD 2010 "Package a Set of Files for Internet Transmission": docs.autodesk.com/ACD/2010/ENU/AutoCAD%202010… — lists *.pmp among file types not added automatically. 7) Autodesk Community forum snippets (search results): forums.autodesk.com/t5/autocad-forum/where-do… (external LIN and custom PAT files "do not tag along"); forums.autodesk.com/t5/autocad-forum/etransmi… (.shx not included by default; use Include Fonts). Contrary evidence considered: a third-party Novedge blog (novedge.com/blogs/design-news/autocad-tip-etr…) states eTransmit "collects PC3/PMP configs"; this contradicts Autodesk's own 2010 and 2027 tables on PMP and is outweighed by the vendor documentation. The Revit page "File Types Not Included When Transmitting a Model" (help.autodesk.com/view/RVT/2027/ENU/?guid=GUI…) is a different product and lists different exclusions (point clouds, shared parameter file, lookup tables, external fonts), so it is not the basis of this AutoCAD claim. Direct page fetches of help.autodesk.com, forums.autodesk.com and the third-party blogs were blocked by the session proxy; vendor text was obtained through the Autodesk Product Help MCP index and web-search snippets.

A.3 Completeness critique

A final worker read all nine research results and asked what was missing, what contradicted what, and what to do next. The contradictions were reconciled in the research notes before this page was generated; the follow-ups are the next iteration's work list and are recorded in the knowledge pack's learnings log.

Angles the critic found missing

  • Carlson Civil/Survey and Trimble Business Center are absent from the comparison grid although they are the perpetual-licence, surveyor-preferred incumbents that Genesis would displace first; only prices were captured (market-and-stability). Civil3d-features or bricscad-features should have added a feature/review snapshot row for Carlson and TBC (and Bentley OpenSite) as reference points.
  • BricsCAD referencing, data connections and recovery depth (the Genesis criteria) is thin: XCLIP, Reference-Manager-style reports, Bricsys 24/7 status, autosave/.bak/RECOVER behaviour, xref load performance and GIS/shapefile import are all 'unverified' or 'unknown'. Owner: bricscad-features.
  • BricsCAD crash volume was never measured: market-and-stability counted Autodesk release-note crash fixes (10-17 per point release) but explicitly skipped Bricsys, even though bricscad-features found the per-build release-notes URL (boa.bricscad.octave.com/common/releasenotes.jsp). Owner: market-and-stability.
  • What BricsCAD Ultimate does with a real Civil 3D drawing (AECC proxies: surfaces, parcels, pipes, labels) is not inventoried beyond 'alignments import'; likewise what CADCraft does with AECC proxy objects (kept through DXF, dropped through DWG per cadcraft-refs) was not tested with a sample file. Owners: bricscad-features, cadcraft-refs.
  • Civil Site Design for BricsCAD (the add-on the market treats as the Civil 3D alternative) was used only as an inference; its feature list, price and reviews were never captured, so the 'BricsCAD civil stack is incomplete' claim rests on marketing copy. Owner: bricscad-features.
  • BricsCAD V27 (launched 2026-10-20, the day before research) is covered only by launch-page bullets; no V27 civil changes, pricing or regressions. Owners: bricscad-features, bricscad-reviews.
  • CADCraft inventory was built from the repo's own docs without cross-checking upstream issues: it omits #540 (silent data loss when two instances save one DWG), #1636 (xref re-path crash), #1001 (lost clicks), #1041 (23-file community corpus, BricsCAD refused 14/23) and #604 (AutoCAD 2027 round trip adds 6 AUDIT errors, downgrades to AC1018). Owner: cadcraft-inventory.
  • No CADCraft system requirements or hardware section (Civil 3D 32 GB/8 GB GPU and BricsCAD 16 GB are given); the only evidence is 4.5-8.4 GB peaks from issues. Owner: cadcraft-inventory.
  • CADCraft governance and sustainability: number of human maintainers, storytold org funding/sponsors, the 'largely agent-written' development model, Discord-relay bug intake and the open trademark issue (#40) are not assessed, yet the doc will argue 'zero crashes' needs a maintained project. Owners: opensource-reviews, market-and-stability.
  • acadrust (the MPL-2.0 DWG bridge CADCraft depends on) is not evaluated: issue count, test corpus, whether it writes DWG natively, status of its PR #147, and whether an ODA-licensed path is even possible for an open-source app. Owner: cadcraft-refs.
  • 'Simpler' is never quantified: no command/sysvar/style-type counts for Civil 3D or BricsCAD to set against CADCraft's 431 commands and ~125 sysvars. Owners: civil3d-features, bricscad-features.
  • Civil 3D's copy-between-drawings behaviour (style-conflict prompt, AECC object copy rules, 'Export Civil 3D Drawing') was not described, although it is the direct analogue of CADCraft's merge rule and of Genesis's 'file copying' goal. Owner: civil3d-features.
  • Patent/IP exposure of AutoCAD-parity features: bricscad-reviews reports V26 dynamic-block authoring is withheld from US users 'because of licensing' but nobody verified why; the same risk applies to CADCraft/Genesis clean-room work. Owners: bricscad-reviews, opensource-reviews.
  • Security of file parsing (DWG/DXF CVEs and Autodesk/Bricsys security advisories 2024-2026, AutoLISP acad.lsp malware) is absent from the stability discussion even though 'input-derived numbers are hostile' is CADCraft's framing. Owner: market-and-stability.
  • Open exchange/container formats beyond LandXML 1.2 are not surveyed for Genesis's 'file containment' decision: IFC 4.3 alignment/terrain, OGC LandInfra/InfraGML, LandXML.org maintenance status, GeoPackage as a project container. Owner: landdev-domain.
  • The 'neo' techniques have no tool landscape: what owner-builders and permaculture designers use today for solar/PV layout and rainwater sizing (Aurora/HelioScope, SAM, pvlib, Ladybug Tools [AGPL, must not be read], QGIS UMEP, OpenSolar) and which are licence-safe to reuse. Owners: landdev-domain, opensource-reviews.
  • Open-source civil tooling beyond FreeCAD Trails and Blender-GIS is missing: QGIS/GRASS/SAGA for TIN/contours/watersheds, PDAL/Entwine/CloudCompare for lidar, OpenDroneMap for topo, PySWMM/EPANET GUIs, open LandXML libraries. Owner: opensource-reviews.
  • Agent/automation surface comparison is unverified on two sides: civil3d-features' claim that Autodesk provides MCP servers for Autodesk Assistant, and whether BricsCAD V27's AI panel executes commands or only answers help questions; CADCraft's MCP is the only documented one. Owners: civil3d-features, bricscad-features.
  • No reproducible cross-product benchmark plan (same files, open time, xref attach time, peak RAM) despite 'faster referencing' being a headline Genesis goal; cadcraft-refs had the clone and could have timed OPEN/XATTACH with cadcraft-cli perf. Owner: cadcraft-refs.
  • Reddit, Hacker News and YouTube evidence is absent from all three review workers (blocked by the sandbox), so practitioner 'would I switch' sentiment is under-represented for every product. Owners: civil3d-reviews, bricscad-reviews, opensource-reviews.

Contradictions between workers (reconciled)

  • CADCraft xref layer overrides: cadcraft-inventory (2.6) says 'unloaded state and xref-layer overrides last only for the session (F2)', while cadcraft-refs shows the test xref_layer_overrides_survive_save_and_reopen_in_dxf_and_dwg (#886) passes and calls the gaps.md F2 row stale; opensource-reviews also lists #886 as fixed. Only the Unloaded state is lost.
  • CADCraft crash record: cadcraft-refs says '#57 is the one crash-adjacent failure users actually reported', but opensource-reviews lists 42 crash-matching issues including #1636 (xref re-path crash), #1724/#1824 (black window on Intel GPUs), #1631 (Metal device-lost) and #1536 (SIGILL on quit).
  • CADCraft DWG verification: cadcraft-inventory says DWG save is 'never verified in AutoCAD with no real-file corpus', whereas opensource-reviews cites #604 (round-tripped through AutoCAD 2027: 0 to 6 AUDIT errors, AC1032 to AC1018) and the #1041 community corpus of 23 office DWGs tested in ODA, ezdxf and BricsCAD V26.
  • CADCraft Releases tab: the opensource-reviews JSON aggregate_ratings row still says 'no entries on Releases tab' while its own key_claims (post fact-check) say eight releases v0.1.0-v0.10.0 exist.
  • CADCraft test count: cadcraft-refs reports 2,110 #[test] functions, cadcraft-inventory 2,184; the fact-check explains 2,110 is crates/ only and 2,207 is the whole workspace, but the doc must pick one wording.
  • CADCraft engine commands: 413 (inventory) vs 431 (fact-check, including 18 macro-generated constraint commands) vs 427 (parity checklist) vs 295 (architecture.md).
  • REFEDIT status: cadcraft-inventory says REFEDIT/REFSET/REFCLOSE work for blocks (#813 landed, partial); opensource-reviews lists '#813 REFEDIT/REFSET/REFCLOSE pending'.
  • Civil 3D Storm and Sanitary Analysis: landdev-domain states Civil 3D 2026 'ships ... Storm and Sanitary Analysis as extensions' (marked verified via the 2026 help page), but civil3d-features says Autodesk discontinued SSA (last build 2024.1.1, does not install against 2026+) and replaced it with cloud Drainage Analysis.
  • Civil 3D AEC Collection price: civil3d-features gives $3,675/yr from autodesk.com's 2027 page; civil3d-reviews and market-and-stability give ~$3,375/yr from a licensing consultancy.
  • Autodesk Flex minimum purchase: civil3d-reviews still says 'from $300 per 100 tokens'; civil3d-features says the 33-token/$99 minimum dates from a mid-2025 small-business update; market-and-stability (fact-checked) says it changed on 4 June 2026.
  • Civil 3D G2 review count: civil3d-reviews says ~300 (one page 390); civil3d-features says ~125 per an aggregator and 194 on a compare page.
  • Civil 3D 2027.1 release date: civil3d-features gives both 4 Aug 2026 (cadpilot/btl-blog) and an AEC blog post dated 20 Oct 2026.
  • BricsCAD maintenance pricing: bricscad-features reports Ultimate renewal EUR 440/yr (official) and separately that the US maintenance page shows EUR 1,176/yr for Ultimate; bricscad-reviews reports Pro maintenance EUR 710/yr from the same official domain, which would make Ultimate cheaper than Pro. US figures also disagree ($441 vs $484; TrustRadius $2,100 perpetual vs EUR 2,640).
  • BricsCAD Apple silicon: bricscad-features states Rosetta 2 only with a native build 'planned' (system-requirements page); bricscad-reviews leaves it 'unresolved' between a forum claim of Intel-only and Wikipedia's 'Apple Silicon compatible'.
  • BricsCAD V26 crash platforms: bricscad-features says user-reported crashes are 'mostly on macOS and Linux', but bricscad-reviews documents the loudest V25.1/V25.2 crash threads on Windows (MTFLAGS=0 workaround, AMD black screens) and a Capterra 1/5 architecture review.
  • BricsCAD Auto Mapping: bricscad-features lists 'Auto Mapping: linework from survey codes' as a V27 addition, while bricscad-reviews cites reseller KTF praising 'Auto Mapping for Civil Points' in its V26 first impressions.
  • BricsCAD civil points: the bricscad-features JSON marks 'Civil points, point groups, description keys, survey database' as status 'full' while its own note says no survey database or least-squares adjustment was found.
  • BricsCAD data links: the bricscad-features .md says 'unknown/could not be verified' but the JSON (editor's later search) says 'full' with DATALINK details; the .md was not reconciled.
  • LandXML in CADCraft: cadcraft-inventory found zero code hits for LandXML, yet landdev-domain cites a LandXML-1.2.xsd at 'getcadcraft/cadcraft tests/tools/civilcraft-test/landxml-1.2/' as a public copy, implying a CADCraft fork or sibling already carries civil test fixtures.

Follow-ups the critic suggested

  • Count crash/hang fixes per build in BricsCAD release notes: open boa.bricscad.octave.com/common/releasenotes.jsp for V26.1.x, V26.2.x and V27.1.05 and tally 'crash', 'hang', 'freeze' entries to set beside Civil 3D's 10-17 per point release.
  • Settle BricsCAD pricing: open bricscad.octave.com/maintenance, bricscad.octave.com/bricscad/ultimate, bricscad.octave.com/bricscad/pro and bricsys.com/en-us/store/bricscad; capture Ultimate and Pro perpetual (EUR and USD), maintenance renewal, annual subscription, and whether V27 changed them.
  • Query 'BricsCAD V26 dynamic block creation not available United States licensing' on forum.bricsys.com and draftingbench.com, then Google Patents for Autodesk dynamic-block patents (e.g. 'dynamic block' assignee Autodesk) to establish the IP reason and its relevance to CADCraft/Genesis.
  • Capture Civil Site Design for BricsCAD: feature list and price from civilsitedesign.com/apps/civil-site-design-fo… (pipes, plan production, labels), plus any G2/forum reviews, to test the 'native civil stack is incomplete' inference.
  • Verify BricsCAD civil/reference commands on help.bricsys.com: XCLIP, CIVILEXPLORER, LANDXMLIMPORT/LANDXMLEXPORT (surfaces), CROSSSECTION/sample lines, GEOGRAPHICLOCATION and CRS library, GeoTIFF/DEM import, GIS panel shapefile import with attributes, Civil 3D import (CIVIL3DIMPORT) scope for surfaces and parcels.
  • Query 'BricsCAD open Civil 3D drawing proxy objects surface alignment pipe network' (forum.bricsys.com, help.bricsys.com) to document what survives when BricsCAD Ultimate opens an AECC drawing.
  • Reconcile SSA: open help.autodesk.com/view/CIV3D/2026/ENU/?guid=G… and the Autodesk KB article 'Cannot find the download for Storm and Sanitary Analysis for Civil 3D' to state exactly which Civil 3D versions can still install SSA.
  • Settle Autodesk list prices: open autodesk.com/products/civil-3d/overview (buy), autodesk.com/collections/architecture-enginee… and autodesk.com/buying/flex/flex-rate-sheet plus the adsknews Flex small-business post to fix Civil 3D annual, AEC Collection annual ($3,375 vs $3,675) and the Flex 33-token minimum date.
  • Open g2.com/products/civil-3d/reviews and g2.com/products/octave-bricscad/reviews to record the live review counts and dates (Civil 3D 125/194/300/390 conflict).
  • In the clone, run `cargo test -p cadcraft-engine xref_layer_overrides_survive_save_and_reopen_in_dxf_and_dwg` and `grep -n 'last only for the session' docs/gaps.md`, then correct the inventory row 2.6 and gaps.md F2.
  • Search GitHub for a civil sibling: `gh search repos civilcraft`, `gh api /orgs/storytold/repos`, and `gh api repos/getcadcraft/cadcraft/contents/tests/tools/civilcraft-test` to confirm the LandXML-1.2.xsd fixture landdev-domain cited; also grep the local plan/ folder for 'civil', 'LandXML', 'TIN'.
  • Run `gh issue list --repo storytold/cadcraft --search crash --state open --limit 100` and `gh issue view` on #540, #1636, #1724, #1631, #1536, #1625, #1076 to record their status as of v0.10.0 (fixed, open, no human reply).
  • Reproduce #540 in the clone: open the same DXF in two `cadcraft` instances, save from both, and check whether v0.10.0 warns, writes a .bak, or detects external modification; grep crates/engine for 'bak', 'fsync', 'modified on disk'.
  • Run `cargo xtask ci` and `cadcraft-cli perf` in the container, then time `cadcraft-cli info` and an XATTACH script on a large public DXF/DWG (e.g. ezdxf's test corpus or a synthetic 500k-entity file) to give first-party open/xref numbers and peak RSS.
  • Assess acadrust: github.com/hakanaktt/acadrust (open issues, test corpus, native DWG write vs DXF bridge, PR #147 status, 0.6.5 changelog) and whether CADCraft has bumped from 0.6.4.
  • Verify 'Autodesk provides MCP servers' for Autodesk Assistant: query 'Autodesk MCP server Civil 3D Assistant' on aps.autodesk.com and help.autodesk.com/view/CONNECT; verify BricsCAD V27 AI chat scope (help-only vs command execution) on bricscad.octave.com/bricscad/v27.
  • Confirm the NREL to NLR host migration (developer.nlr.gov vs developer.nrel.gov, retirement 2026-05-29) and PVWatts v8/NSRDB rate limits, since every solar endpoint in landdev-domain depends on it.
  • Check NOAA Atlas 15 status and the Atlas 14 scripted-access policy at water.noaa.gov/about/atlas15 and weather.gov/owp/hdsc_faqs.
  • Add Carlson Civil/Survey and Trimble Business Center snapshots: features and pricing from carlsonsw.com and trimble.com, reviews from G2/Capterra and rpls.com ('Carlson vs Civil 3D'), so the grid has the surveyor's perpetual incumbent.
  • Survey licence-safe solar/rain libraries for Genesis: pvlib-python (BSD-3), NREL SAM/PySAM (BSD), Reda-Andreas SPA paper; confirm Ladybug Tools is AGPL (do not read); list commercial comparators (Aurora, HelioScope, PVsyst) and QGIS UMEP.
  • Survey open civil exchange formats: buildingSMART IFC 4.3 (ISO 16739-1:2024) alignment/terrain support, OGC LandInfra/InfraGML status, LandXML.org liveness and 2.0 status, GeoPackage as a project container.
  • Retrieve Reddit sentiment through an alternative route (Pullpush API or old.reddit via the proxy) for r/civil3d 'crash 2026', r/Surveying 'Carlson vs Civil 3D', r/bricscad 'switched', r/cad 'CADCraft'.
  • Collect DWG/DXF parsing security advisories 2024-2026 from autodesk.com/trust/security-advisories and NVD searches for 'BricsCAD' and 'AutoCAD DWG' to support the hostile-input framing.
  • Verify open-source funding leads: ondsel.com/blog/goodbye/ (shutdown date and reason), fpa.freecad.org finances, qcad.org/en/shop prices, fund.krita.org and fund.godotengine.org income figures.
  • Query 'BricsCAD V27 Apple silicon native' and the V27 system-requirements page to close the Rosetta question; query 'Bricsys 24/7 Octave 2026' to confirm whether the cloud DMS still exists after the rebrand.
  • Confirm Bentley perpetual availability for OpenRoads/OpenSite on bentley.com licensing pages (G2's $15,142 perpetual is third-party) before stating Bentley is a remaining perpetual vendor.
  • Open help.autodesk.com for Civil 3D 'style conflict' and 'copying objects between drawings' (AECCDWGSETUP, Drawing Settings) to document Civil 3D's merge rules against CADCraft's clipboard rule.
  • Record CADCraft project health: `gh api /orgs/storytold/members`, GitHub Sponsors/Open Collective presence, commit authors over the last 30 days (`git shortlog -sn --since=2026-09-21`), and the status of trademark issue #40.
  • Count Civil 3D and BricsCAD command/sysvar/style-type totals (Civil 3D Command Reference index, BricsCAD command reference index, Civil 3D style types list) to quantify 'simpler' against CADCraft's 431 commands.

A.4 Numbers the Genesis targets depend on

Section 4 states targets. None of them is a measurement of Genesis, which does not exist; each is derived from a measurement or a report listed here.

TargetWhere it comes from
Warm re-reference of a 50 MB base under 1 sField reports of a 47.8 MB DWG taking 20–28 s and 4.5–5.1 GB (upstream #1076) and a 76.8 MB xref freezing at 8.4 GB (#1625); the copy-on-write store makes a cache hit a pointer copy, so the target is the cost of a hash check
Cold open of a 10 MB DWG under 10 sA 10 MB DWG failed after about two minutes on 0.6.x (#57); the open-source review worker's wish list asks for first paint under about 12 s on 20–50 MB drawings
Peak memory under 2.5 GB on a 50 MB drawingReviewers' wish (#1076, #1625, #738); requires streaming parsers instead of the DXF-text bridge, which inflates a DWG 5–6×
No UI stall over 100 ms in the benchmark suiteHuman-perception threshold used in CADCraft's own input-latency discussion (#1001 lost clicks); the engine already re-indexes an edit in 16.6 ms on 200,000 entities
Permit-ready plan set in under two working days for a trained drafterSet by us as the simplicity measure; to be calibrated with the design partners in phase 2
60 core commands cover 90 % of site work; about 100 civil commands in allOur scoping decision, derived from the ten workflow stages in 4.2; Civil 3D's command reference runs to several hundred civil commands
Zero crashes in the field, published monthlyAutodesk's own fixed-issue lists carry 12–17 crash or hang fixes per Civil 3D release; CADCraft's 42 crash-tagged issues on 0.6–0.10 are the baseline to beat
1,350–2,200 agent-hours in seven phasesCADCraft's published calibration (docs/target-app-parity.md), which the project itself says has run optimistic for depth work

CAppendix C: notes and trademarks

  • Trademarks. AutoCAD and Civil 3D are trademarks of Autodesk, Inc.; BricsCAD is a trademark of Bricsys NV, which now trades as Octave (a Hexagon spin-off). They are named here only to identify the products being compared. No Autodesk or Bricsys artwork, icons, fonts, patterns, screenshots or documentation text appear in this document or in the CADCraft repository.
  • Prices change. Every price is quoted with the page it was read from on 2026-10-21 and with the plan name; regional prices, promotions and resellers differ.
  • Reviews are opinions. Review themes are paraphrased summaries of what several reviewers wrote; the short quotations inside them are attributed to the site, thread or issue in the source column and in the research notes. Ratings are the sites' own figures on the date read.
  • Genesis is a plan, not a product; its column in every table marks targets, and its estimates follow the calibration the CADCraft project publishes for AI-agent work (docs/target-app-parity.md), which has so far proven optimistic for depth work.
  • Keeping this page current. Regenerate it from the research notes and the repository; update the revision table in docs/genesis/README.md when numbers change.
